| Toxic Precision | Venomist + Surgeon | Sever adds 3 poison stacks |
| Menagerie | Covenant + Grafter | your creatures carry your newest graft's power (hand: harder blows, legs: faster, heart: they mend, brow: blows stagger, throat: blows splash, back: blows poison) |
| Scald | Tidecaller + Flame Contractor | your burning ground under a soaked foe bursts into steam: every foe within 70 loses track of you for 3 seconds and is scalded for 4, and the foe dries off (`main.gd _update_flames` calls `Synergies.scald`) |
| Lightning Steel | Stormcaller + Blade Forger | a conjured blade that strikes charges its foe, and lightning leaps from it to your nearest other blade in flight within 180 (or, with none, the nearest foe), striking and charging every foe along the bolt for 3 (`projectile.gd` calls `Synergies.conduct`) |

Tests: `tests/parts/reactions.gd` (every reaction from real casts, each named synergy and the lent traits, the Unbound's states, old saves, the climber side on and its switch, the book, the Library and the Entity's words); `tests/parts/consumables.gd` (each state from its monster, on the HUD, cured and warded by its items, and fair).

## Core essences (Making and Unmaking) and the aura

Two essences aren't like the six (`scripts/secret.gd`, where every word and number lives). **Ordinary monsters never leave them: only great foes do.** A tower's beast leaves mostly Making and some Unmaking; the Proving's shadow of the beast the same split, but less; a champion (a sealed wing's, or the Proving's sentinel) a little of one or the other, by tower (Making from the Kraken's, Manticore's and Firebird's towers, Unmaking from the Gryphon's, Wyvern's and Chimera's). A mirror image of the climber (see **Mirror images**) leaves mostly Unmaking and some Making. Amounts grow with the floor like other essence. None drop on the bondless first climb. They rise from the foe as big glowing motes in their colour (Making green, Unmaking violet) and fly to you. They aren't currency: the crystals, the store, the bench and density never list them.

**Choosing a path.** The first time a great foe falls and its secret essence rises (usually the Proving's shadow on floor 6), the game stops on a choice: let in **Making** ("you endure") or **Unmaking** ("you take apart"). The Entity says what's at stake, and each card shows a climber standing in that essence's shadow and what each step does. The choice is for good, so a card is taken with a **second** press or tap (the first only picks it, and presses in the first moment are ignored, so a tap meant for the fight can't choose). It's saved with the climber; later, only the Developer mark or pleading with a Maker will be able to change it (`main.gd reset_path()` is the hook; the dev tools use it now).

**Only your path's essence soaks in.** The other still rises from great foes and visibly streaks through you and out the far side, unbanked, unless you carry a **vessel** (below).

**The vessel** (`scripts/vessel.gd`, where its rules, numbers and words live; its name, "Vessel", is a placeholder in `items.gd`; `scripts/vessel_page.gd` puts it on the train). A sealed glass vessel bound in brass, drawn in code: a round glass belly in brass bands with a sealed domed cap, filling with what it holds (Making green, Unmaking violet; a full one glows and glints). It's rare: a **Rune Artist brews one at the bench** (the Vessel counter: 60 Iron, 45 Water, 45 Air, `BREW`), and very rarely (**4%**, `VAULT_CHANCE`) a vault's full prize offers one as its third card (never on the first climb, nor while you carry one). It sits on the belt like other items (one kind of item, never stacked, never swapped out by the belt's take card), hangs at the climber's hip glowing with what it holds, and while you carry one a small vessel and gauge sit beside the health bar (hover for what it holds). Using it only says what it holds.
- **Capturing.** Once you have a path, the other secret essence that rises from a great foe streaks at you as before, but curls round the vessel at your hip and sinks into it ("+12 Unmaking into the vessel (12 / 30)"). It holds **30** (`CAPACITY`), of one kind (the first it took); whatever doesn't fit passes through you as before. Your path's essence soaks into you as always, and before a path is chosen it catches nothing.
- **Unbanked, until home.** Fall and the vessel and all it holds are lost with the belt (the fall screen says so). Survive the climb or read a recall scroll and it comes home, filled or not, into your **storage chest** (after the chest's pieces; it's never broken into crystals with the rest of the belt). A vessel's card in the chest packs it for your next climb (**Take it on your next climb**: it goes on your belt as you depart, takes a belt kind, and keeps filling) or leaves it.
- **A full vessel**, three uses, each the player's choice (the owner hasn't picked one yet):
  - **Trade it** at the store: the store's **Sell** section lists your vessels, and a full one trades for **120 crystal essence of one kind you choose**, or **30 of every kind** (180 in all) (`TRADE`). Each button asks once.
  - **Work it into a piece** at the bench (the **Vessel** counter; crafted: free for a climber with the Rune Artist's mark, a small fee for the Rune Artist otherwise): **temper a +3 piece to +4**, one rank past the bench's limit (each rank still 25% of its traits' base and 4% for a weapon; `gear.gd RANK_CAP`), or cut a **deep rune**, a lasting rune at the Inscribe spell's full strength, twice a bench rune: Sharpness +20% strikes, Warding +16% protection, Swiftness +10% walk (`gear.gd LASTING_RUNES "deep_*"`; it replaces any rune there, and gear's caps still apply). Either uses up a full vessel from your chest.
  - **Offer it.** Carried up full on your belt, a Maker's meeting adds a card, **Offer the vessel**: a plea for a change of path that's likelier granted (+35%, at most 95%; `makers.gd PLEA_ODDS`). The Maker keeps the vessel whether it agrees or not (`vessel_back` true would hand it back on a refusal). And on the train the Developer's **bribe** can offer a full vessel from your chest, likelier taken than crystals (+25%, at most 90%; `secret_bonds.gd BRIBE`); like any bribe, nothing is taken if it refuses.
- The Entity explains the first vessel, the first capture and the first full vessel. Vessels in the chest are saved with the train (`train`/`vessels`; old saves load with none). The dev tools (Secret essences) give an empty or a full vessel.

**Core essence** (`scripts/core.gd`, every word and number). Your path's essence never takes a mark and never pushes out a bond. You carry it unbanked during the climb (half of it stays when the Proving casts you down; a fall or a recall scroll loses it), and at the stair **half of it (`RATE`) settles in your body as core essence**: much more slowly than other essence, only from great foes, **spread evenly over the six body parts** (`profile.flow` is the body's total). Each part holds **120** (`CAP`, shared with the Unbound's cups), so core essence **stops once the body is saturated** (720 for a marked climber; for the Unbound, whatever room their own essence leaves). What comes after saturation, replacing other essence until the whole body is core essence (**Ascendance**), is only a "Coming soon" line on the Essence tab and in the Entity's words for now. The dev tools' "+25 / +100 flow" add that much core essence straight away.

**The aura** is the core essence made visible, for marked climbers and the Unbound alike: a soft halo around the climber (drawn unlit over the darkness: `secret.gd draw_aura`, from `player.gd`'s overlay), a living green glow that swells with a heartbeat and sheds rising spores for Making, a flickering violet haze that sheds drifting shards for Unmaking, brighter and wider at each step, and from step 4 a slow ring of light turning around you. Its **six steps** come at **12, 36, 72, 126, 198 and 300** core essence (`secret.gd STEPS`), each a real change in play (today's Making and Unmaking bonuses, spread over six steps):

| Step | Making | Unmaking |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Health regrows: 0.5 a second | 12% more damage (strikes and spells), 8% less max health (16/10%, 20/13%, 26/16%, 31/19%, 36/22% at later steps) |
| 2 | Every kill mends you: 4 health; regrowth 0.65 | Your aura erodes awake monsters beside you: 3 damage a second |
| 3 | Regrowth 0.8, mending 4.5 | Erosion 3.5, and the trade grows |
| 4 | Healing is 50% stronger: Healing Vials, springs, your marks' heals, weapons that heal on a kill; regrowth 0.95, mending 5 | Your strikes ignore armour: a shellback's shell doesn't turn them, a knight's guard doesn't shrug off light blows (beasts still don't flinch); erosion 4 |
| 5 | Everything grows again: regrowth 1.1, mending 5.5, healing 55% | Erosion 4.5, and the trade grows again |
| 6 | Once a floor, a killing blow leaves you at a quarter of your health instead, in a green burst that throws back the monsters near you ("Making will not let you fall"); regrowth 1.25, mending 6, healing 60% | A slain foe comes apart: 30% of its max health (at most 60) to the monsters beside it (an undone foe doesn't undo again); erosion 5 |

**It lives in your shadow.** With a path, the shadow under the climber holds it, drawn in code and growing darker, larger and more alive (or more broken) at every step. Making: the shadow turns living green and pulses with a heartbeat, sending a bright ring out with each beat; from step 2 roots reach past its edge, from step 3 small leaves sway at their tips and trail behind you as you move, and grass-light flickers where it falls (a soft green light at your feet). Unmaking: the shadow goes violet with a splintered edge, cracks like fractured glass, its edges flaking into rising motes; from step 3 it drinks the light (the darkness layer gathers in a ring around you). Before the first step it's only a faint tint.

**On screen.** Your path's gem appears beside the health bar (with six pips for the aura's steps; hover it for what they do) only once the aura reaches its first step, and then your unbanked flow leads the essence counter. Before that, the HUD shows nothing and the climber screen only a faint sign. The climber screen's **Essence** tab shows the aura (your figure in its aura and shadow, the step, what each of the six does, the core essence in your body, how much to the next, what you carry unbanked, and once saturated, "Ascendance: coming soon"), and on the **Marks** tab the figure stands in its shadow. The Entity explains the core essences, the choice, the other essence passing through, core essence settling, the aura, saturation and each step the first time, and "How do things work here?" has "Core essence, and my aura?".

## The Unbound

Climbers who refuse the tower beasts' marks (`scripts/unbound.gd` holds every word and number about them and their profile state; `scripts/technique.gd` the techniques; `scripts/unbound_run.gd` runs them in a climb; `scripts/technique_tab.gd` is their Techniques tab on the climber screen; `scripts/technique_page.gd` their part cards and the Developer's way back; `scripts/body_move.gd` moves essence on the climber screen's Body tab). Old saves load as marked climbers.

**Becoming Unbound.** It happens only before your first mark, and only once. **Don't break the rules and you are offered:** at the Regard, dismissing it asks first, **accept the mark** or **refuse it**. Refusing takes a second press (and none in the first moment), since there's no road back; the mark the beast just gave is taken back. **Break the rules and you are refused:** with your **fists out** (key 1), hold a strike against a barrier: a **vault's seal** or a **lock-in room's shut doorway**. (The stair's own barrier, its gate or ward, works differently: anyone may slip past or break it, for a horde, and with no mark it makes you Unbound at once; see **The climb**.) The very first time ever, it only shudders with a faint crack (the warning; the Entity says what breaking one means). After that each held blow (0.45 s) drains 4 unbanked essence, hurts your hands for 5 (10 with almost nothing to drain; never the last of your health) and cracks it: 4 blows break a ward or a seal, 3 a doorway. Breaking through skips the challenge and forfeits its reward (the stair opens with no puzzle counted and no puzzle bonus; a vault stands open and empty, its room's question ended). **One barrier broken on the first climb breaks the tower's rules:** at the Regard (reached or fallen) the beast **refuses** to mark you: a "REFUSED" screen, no mark and no choice, and you are Unbound. **After the first climb** a marked climber can't become Unbound: their fists only make a barrier shudder ("It shudders, and holds"), and summits never offer a refusal. The Unbound still break barriers later (the Proving's sealed reliquary too) with raw essence (below). Old saves' barrier counts are dropped when they load. Being Unbound is for good, except through the **Developer**: its holder gets a "Mark me again" button on the Core tab (on the train, two presses), which puts the densest part's raw essence into a bond there (chosen by what's pooled in it) and keeps half of the rest as soak, so later marks come sooner. Raw essence against barriers (the Unbound): from a step away with any melee weapon or fists, 6 unbanked essence a blow, no hurt, two cracks a blow.

**Nodes** (`scripts/unbound.gd`, every rule and number; the profile's `part_density` holds what each Node holds, the Unbound state's `size` each Node's size). The Unbound's six body parts are called **Nodes** everywhere the player reads it (the Hand Node, Legs Node, Heart Node, Brow Node, Throat Node and Back Node; marked climbers keep "body parts"). A Node has **two numbers**: what it **holds** (essence by kind: it powers the Node's techniques and can be moved) and its **size**, its **density** (how much it can hold, 120 at most). It never holds more than its size: banked essence that doesn't fit condenses into crystals (essence is never lost). **Asleep:** every Node starts asleep, at size 0, holding nothing, and **the player sees no numbers for it** (the cards, the Tree, the Techniques tab, the figure and the summit's cards all say only "asleep"). Banked essence flows to its kind's natural Node (Blade the hand, Air the legs, Stone the back, Water the throat, Fire the brow, Iron the heart), or wherever it's **routed** at the train, and **soaks in**: 10 more size cost 60 banked essence at level 0, then 90 and 120 (`GROW_COST`); moving essence into a sleeping Node feeds it the same way. At **size 30** it **awakens** (level 1, its minor ability). **Awake, it grows only by use** (the body getting stronger with training): what its technique spends on a cast (its fill, times `USE` "cast" 2) and the essence its boosts pay (times 2), or before it has a technique its own action (what would fill one, times 0.5), is asked back at the next stair (`note_use`); the essence banked into that Node first tops up what it holds, then refills that use (used up, not crystals), and each 10 refilled size costs 143 from size 30, then 179, 246, 355, 497, 687, 950, 1330 and 1871 (`GROW_COST`). **Use not refilled at its stair is gone**, so casting more than you bank adds nothing. **The one rule** (the same as for marks, `mark_use.gd`): a Node's refill is paid in full by its own kinds (its natural kind and the essence that leads it); any other kind banked into it (routed there, or flowing on from a sealed Node) pays only a third (`USE` "other", `pay_rate`), so routing helps but no longer doubles the pace; a sleeping Node soaks any kind in full (routing wakes Nodes). There is **no cap a climb**: what you bank (refills cost it) and strain (pushing techniques builds strain toward an eruption) hold the pace. Moving essence never changes a size. A typical climber's main Node wakes after about one climb, the others after 3 to 11 (2 to 6 when they route essence to them); one Node reaches level 12 after about 22 typical climb attempts (8 summiting every climb) and all six after about 72, travelling to the tower whose essence feeds the smallest Node (the owner's pace, so climbs don't grow repetitive) (`tests/progression_model.gd run_unbound`; `tests/parts/unbound_cup.gd` holds the pace to its bands; a spammer is no faster). **Old saves:** an Unbound from before the Nodes has each part's fill (own essence and core) made its Node's size, so no growth is lost; a part under 30 sleeps, keeping that size toward waking, and the essence it held condenses into crystals. **The first essence in a Node leads it (its primary)** and must stay at least half of the Node's own essence: another essence that would push it under can't come in; **three kinds at most** a Node. The others are **modifiers**: each adds its touch to every technique of the Node, at twice its share (at most full strength). Your path's core essence (`core.gd`) takes room in every body part beside its Node (each part holds 120 of the two together), adds its own touch to every technique (at twice its share of all the part holds, at most 75%) and counts for neither rule. A sleeping Node is level 0; it **wakes at level 1** (size 30) and reaches **level 12 at size 120**, the levels set by size (`LEVELS`: 30, 38, 46, 54, 62, 70, 78, 86, 94, 102, 111, 120; growth is priced by size, so the levels don't change the pace): **level 1** is waking, with the Node's **minor ability** (now and then what the Node does, a weapon strike for the hand, a roll for the legs, a parry for the brow, a blow survived for the heart, a swing catching several foes for the throat, a backstab or finisher for the back, carries a touch of its lead essence to the nearest foe: `MINOR`, 35% a time, at most every 1.5 s), **level 3** a **young technique** (below), **level 5** its **gift** (hand: strikes 15% harder; legs: walk 8% faster; heart: 15% more health; brow: techniques cost 25% less; throat: stamina back 20% faster; back: strain fades twice as fast), **level 6** the **full technique**, then the tree's forks and forms, and **level 12** (size 120) its **capstone**, and its **signature** if one essence alone fills it (a pure Node: pure Air in the Legs Node brings **Blink**, a short vanishing step to where you aim, untouchable a moment, striking foes where you land; the other Nodes' signatures are named but show as "Coming soon"). Techniques hit harder as the Node grows: 60% at level 3 (young), 1x at level 6, twice as hard at 12. **Attributes** (Agility, Strength, Vitality, Will, Intellect, Spirit) come from the Nodes' sizes, not banked density: each awake Node's size counts for the essences it holds, in proportion to how much of each (`attr_size`; all of it to its natural essence while it holds none), and a bonus is half its cap at 60 size (`ATTR`); the Workshop's tempering asks for that same size (`profile.gd body_density`). The Nodes' total size sets the **stage**: Stir, Pulse, Current, Surge, Swell, Torrent, Tide, Tempest, Deluge, Maelstrom, Cataclysm, Unbound (twelve, at 0 to 720), shown on the climber screen. They never take tower bonds; secret bonds still come from the Makers. At a **summit** they take no mark: a card screen lets them **condense** 60 of the tower's essence into a Node of their choice (`CONDENSE`): a sleeping Node soaks it in; an awake one grows as that much refilled use would (outside the climb's cap) and tops up what it holds; then the victory.

**Sealing a Node** (`scripts/unbound.gd` `seal`, `unseal`, `SEAL`; the Body tab's section in `scripts/node_seal.gd`). The Unbound choose; the marked can't. **On the train only**, under the chosen Node's levels on the Body tab, an Unbound can **seal** it. A clear warning (in the warning colour) says what's lost, and **Seal** asks first (the confirmation pop-up, with the warning; **Unseal** asks too). A sealed Node **takes no essence**: banked essence that would pool there (its natural kind, or a kind routed there) flows on to the biggest open Node, where, as another kind, it pays only a third of a refill (the one rule, below); routing skips sealed Nodes, and moving essence never targets one. Sealing **gives the Node up**: what it holds condenses into crystals, and its size, technique button, gift, minor ability and attributes are gone (its core essence moves to the open parts). **At least one Node stays open.** Each sealed Node makes the open ones stronger: **+4% growth from refilled use** (`SEAL` "growth") and **+6% technique power** (`SEAL` "power") each, adding up. **Unsealing** is allowed (on the train, one press): the Node starts again **asleep at size 0**. The agreed pace: focus trades breadth for depth, not speed: with one Node open (the other five sealed after the first climb) it reaches level 12 after about 16 typical climb attempts, against a broad climber's 22 (`tests/progression_model.gd run_unbound` with `sealed`; held by `tests/parts/sealing.gd`). A sealed Node shows an iron seal crossed by two bands on the figure and the Tree (drawn with the shading toolkit), "sealed" on its card, its Techniques tab slot, the Tree's heading and the Abilities list; the summit's condensing skips it. The Entity explains it the first time the Body tab offers sealing, when you seal and when you unseal, in "How does it work?" ("Sealing a Node?"), and on both buttons when hovered or held. Old saves have nothing sealed.

**A fall drains the Nodes** (`scripts/unbound.gd` `drain`, `DRAIN`). The guardians hunt the Unbound. When an Unbound falls (after the first climb), **67% of every essence each Node holds drains back into the well** (lost: not crystals), each Node keeping at least a trace (1) of its lead essence, so its lead, technique and minor ability stay the same. **Sizes, levels and the core essence are kept.** The next stairs' banked essence tops the Nodes up again before it refills their use, so growth is a little slower for a climb. The fall screen's first line says it ("Caught and drained: N essence from your Nodes runs back into the well..."). Why two thirds and not all of it: the pace model (`tests/progression_model.gd run_unbound`, which now drains at every fall) puts all six Nodes at level 12 after 79 climbs with a full drain, past the owner's 66 to 78; with two thirds it is 77 (the main Node: 23 climbs, 22 without any drain; summiting every climb: 8, unchanged). The Entity explains it the first time ("Drained", with a "What happens when I fall?" topic), and after the third drained fall speaks of **the Husks**: climbers say the husks are Unbound drained so often that they were hollowed out (the Bestiary's husk entry says so too). Old saves lose nothing (a count of drained falls is kept from now on). Tests: `tests/parts/sworn.gd`, the pace in `tests/parts/unbound_cup.gd` and `tests/parts/sealing.gd`.

**One of the Sworn steps in** (`scripts/sworn.gd` `try_rescue`, `RESCUE`). The Makers admire and fear the Unbound, and send their **Sworn** (climbers sworn to a Maker by its secret bond) to stop captures. **About 1 in 6** times a blow would fell a hunted Unbound (`main.gd proving_spare` asks the Proving, a station's arena and a duel first), **at most once a climb**, never on the first climb, one of the Sworn steps in: a cloaked figure with its Maker's sign beside the climber for a few seconds (drawn with the shading toolkit), a banner ("ONE OF THE SWORN": who sent them, and a line in their voice, using the climber's pronouns), the climber stands again at **35%** health, untouchable for **2 seconds**, and the foes within 170 reel back (half as long for a guardian). The Maker most taken with the climber (its interest) is the one whose Sworn comes. Only for the Unbound. The Entity explains it the first time ("One of the Sworn").

**Moving essence** (`scripts/body_move.gd`: the climber screen's **Body** tab, the Unbound's Marks tab). It's the body's own doing, so it works **wherever the climber screen opens**: on the train, at a camp, at a stair, in a climb; but **not while a monster is after you** (one that has noticed you, awake and not lost, `body_move.gd hunted`): then Move says "Wait: a monster is after you" and the line under it explains. The Body tab shows a card for each Node (asleep: only that it sleeps; awake: its level, its size of 120, what it holds by kind, its core, the size to its next level and its technique), and each card is a button: **press a card** to choose that Node (a gold edge marks it): under the cards, **what each of its levels brings** (its Tree's nodes in plain words, the reached ones bright; a sleeping Node says only how it wakes), then the **attributes** the Nodes give, then Move essence, where an awake Node becomes the one to move out of and a sleeping one the one to feed. **From** (an awake Node, or press a card), **Essence** (which of its essences), **To** (the Node it goes into: by default the first other Node that can take it, never a drain; pressing it skips the awake Nodes that can't: full, three kinds already, or its lead would fall under half; a sleeping Node always can, "asleep: feed it", and what arrives soaks in) and **Move** (up to `MOVE_STEP`, 6, a press; the button shows the amount and the price). Right under the control a line says what Move will do or, when it can't, why (`unbound.gd why_not`), then the result of the last press, and the cards show the change at once. **Drain into crystals** is a separate button: it takes the essence out of the part into crystals of its kind. **A quarter of what you move (or drain) is the price** (`MOVE_COST`), and essence is never lost: before a path it condenses into crystals, on the Making path it turns into core essence (spread evenly), on the Unmaking path it is unmade. Each button explains itself when hovered or held, and the Entity explains the Body tab the first time it opens ("Your body") and moving the first time you move. The Techniques tab beside it keeps only a line and a **Body tab** button.

**Core-led parts.** Drain a part's own essence until its core essence is at least half of what it holds and **the core leads it**: its technique becomes a **core technique** (its own name, `CORE_TECHS`, placeholders for the owner: Mending Hands, Hollow Step...), led by Making or Unmaking, hitting 1.5x as hard from level 6 and 2x when the part is full (`CORE_POWER`), each cast thrown out in a big burst of the core's own colours. The part's other essence adds only a small touch (25%), and at each stair the core absorbs a sixth of it (`core.gd ABSORB`) until the part is all core.

**Techniques.** **A Node at level 3 carries one technique, on its own mark button** (Q E F C X V; LB + a face button; the cast buttons on a phone). A new Unbound starts with none: the essence already in their body soaks into its natural Nodes (the first climb's is enough to nearly wake one), and a Node that reaches level 3 **learns its first form by itself** (its first starting form: the hand's Strike, the legs' Dash...) so it can be cast at once. From level 3 to 5 it's a **young technique**, limited: that first form only, its lead essence only (no modifiers and no core touch), at 60% strength rising to full (`YOUNG_POWER`, `tech_young`); at **level 6** it comes into its own: the Node's other essences join, its other starting form opens, and it hits at full strength. **A technique is a form and the effects of its part's essence**: the part gives the form (the hand a **strike** or a thrown **bolt**, the legs a **dash** (untouchable, striking what you pass) or a **leap**, the back a **shell** or a **push**, the throat a **shout** in a cone or a **pull**, the brow a **mark** (the foe takes 50% more damage) or **sight** (every foe shows through walls and fog), the heart something on yourself), and the essences in the part, in order, add what they do: Fire burns, Water slows (or heals you over time), Air reaches farther and throws harder (and carries a dash farther), Stone staggers and guards, Iron pierces armour and drags foes to you, Blade cuts deeper and bleeds, Making heals you, Unmaking ignores armour and erodes at a small cost in health. Its name comes from the two (Fire and the fists: **Flame Fist**; `technique.gd FIRST_NAMES`). Its essences and their weights are worked out from the part each time it changes (`unbound.gd sync_tech`), so a technique is never out of step with its part. **Each technique fills from its body part's actions**, the way a mark's bond fills (`player.gd _part_action`: landed strikes fill the hand, rolls through attacks the legs, hits survived the heart, parries the brow, one action hitting several foes the throat, backstabs and finishers the back; Water density and fill gear help as for marks). It's **full at 20, 28 or 36** (by how many kinds; `CHARGE`, the brow's gift cuts it a quarter): full, a cast spends nothing you carry. Cast it before it's full and the missing share comes out as up to **24 extra strain** (`EMPTY_STRAIN`). **Carried essence boosts a cast:** if you carry its essences **unbanked** (3, 6 or 8 by how many kinds, in proportion with the lead counting double; the path's essence from your unbanked flow), the cast spends them and hits **50% harder** (`BOOST`), with "boosted" over your head; with less than all of it, nothing is spent. It's automatic, and a switch on the Techniques tab keeps your essence for banking instead. Fill is kept from floor to floor. **The Unbound's climber screen has its own tabs:** **Body** (the Nodes), **Techniques** and **Essence**, then the Tree, Record and Deeds as for everyone (no Abilities and no Marks: "Abilities", the peek and the Climber button open their Techniques tab). Every technique they carry, what each Node's minor ability and gift do, and on the train "Try your abilities" (the practice room) are on the **Techniques** tab, which also chooses the form, the essences' effects and a variation (below, under the tree), each Node's slot, its name, boost and the routing. Their **Essence** tab shows only their aura, the core essence in their body, their crystals by kind (with Crystals and tinctures) and what they carry unbanked: no density, since their Nodes' sizes are on the Body tab. The Entity explains a sleeping Node, a Node awakening, a Node growing with use, level 3 (a young technique), level 6 (the full technique), a first technique learned, level 12, pure, core-led, the Body and Essence tabs and moving essence the first time, and "How do things work here?" has "How do the Unbound grow?", "What are Nodes?", "Forms, effects, techniques?" (a glossary: form, effect, technique, variation) and "The Techniques tab?". 

**Strain.** Each cast strains the technique's part: 14, 22 or 32 (by how many kinds). Above **70** its techniques hit 25% harder but may **waver** (35%: half strength, off aim). At **100** it **erupts**: you lose 45% of your health (**it can kill you**, a real fall), the part goes **dark for 8 seconds** (nothing from it can be cast) and raw essence shows on it. Strain fades (9 a second) once you haven't cast from that part for 1.5 seconds, and the stair's rest clears it. On the HUD each technique's medallion ring is its **fill** (bright when full, like a mark's) with its part's strain bar under it, a white tick at 70; on a phone the cast button's ring fills the same way and its colour runs hot with strain. A Vial of Essence fills techniques as it fills marks.

**How they look.** Instead of marks, **veins of raw essence** spread from each awake Node on the climber, in the colour of what leads it, more and brighter with its level (none on a sleeping Node) (with a soft glow over the darkness); strain makes them flicker hot, a dark part goes grey with raw essence circling it. The climber screen's figure shows the same veins (a faint seed on a sleeping Node), with each Node's level or "asleep".

**Dev tools** (the dev page's "The Unbound"): become Unbound or go back, show the refusal, on a first climb "Break a barrier" / "Unbreak the barrier" (whether the Regard will refuse you), make a Node a level bigger (filled with its lead, or its natural kind), add 40 strain to a part. The Entity explains becoming Unbound, the refusal, the first barrier (the warning), fists, rules broken, a barrier that holds after the first climb, strain, an eruption, techniques, condensing, a sleeping Node, a Node awakening, growing, level 3, level 6, level 12, pure, core-led and moving essence the first time.

## The tree, the library and the book

How marked climbers and the Unbound see what they have and choose how they grow. Every choice is **for good**.

**The library** (`scripts/library.gd`): one shared set of pieces, in data (every name, line and number is in the Data editor: "The library, the tree and the book" in Words, "The library: forms, effects, variations, forks" in Numbers). **Forms** are the shapes body parts give (the eleven first ones: Strike, Bolt, Dash, Leap, Shell, Push, Shout, Pull, Mark, Sight, Within, and two more per part, all written: `unbound_forms.gd`); **effects** are what an essence does (a base one per essence, and three more per essence, all written: `unbound_effects.gd`); **variations** are small alterations shared by both paths: **Wide** (wider, a little weaker), **Long** (reaches farther), **Quick** (winds up in half the time, a little weaker), **Split** (three smaller copies fan out), **Lingering** (leaves a field for 3 seconds), **Chain** (jumps to one more foe), **Delayed** (a moment later, harder), **Mirrored** (also behind you), **Echo** (again, weaker, a second later), **Charged** (a longer wind-up, much harder). Each adds 4 strain to a technique. Every entry has an id, a name, one plain line, its numbers (and per-level changes) and its strain. A piece marked `"placeholder": true` isn't written yet: the tree shows it as **Coming later** and it can't be chosen, so the game works at every stage. The Unbound's techniques (`technique.gd`) read their shapes and kinds from the library and play exactly as before.

**Echoes:** certain mixes come close to a bond's spell, never the same (`library.gd ECHOES`, and ten more in `unbound_effects.gd ECHOES`: 16 across 14 bonds). A recipe is a form, the essence that leads and any it needs among the others: an Air shout with Stone echoes **Gale Lift**, an Air bolt with Iron **Piercing Shot**, a Water shell with Stone **Barrier**, a Water push **Tidal Surge**, a Fire bolt with Air **Fireball**, an Air strike with Fire **Chain Lightning**, a Water pull with Iron **Undertow**, a Stone leap with Iron **Quake**, a Fire strike with Blade **Kindle Blade**, a Blade bolt with Iron **Conjured Blades**, a Fire sight with Air **Smoke Veil**, an Iron mark with Stone **Challenge**, a Making within with Fire **Rekindle**, a Fire dash with Air **Wingburst**, a Water bolt with Blade **Venom Spit** and a Blade mark with Air **Track**. No ready-made technique echoes: they're found by experimenting. An echo adds a weaker touch of the spell after the technique's own cast (`ECHO_ACTS`, at 60% of its power: Gale Lift's echo lifts the light foes in front for 0.7 s, Undertow's drinks a little from the nearest foe...) and costs **6 more strain** a cast. A technique's card says "echoes Gale Lift", the bench's plaque says what the echo adds, and the first cast of one (or setting one at the bench) records it for the climber (`tree.gd note_echo`; the bench counts "Echoes found: 3 of 16").

**The Unbound's effects** (`scripts/spells/unbound_effects.gd`): three for each essence beside its base one, each opening at 12, 36 and 72 of that essence in the body (its fill across the six parts; the path's essence: the core essence). Chosen at the bench for an essence in a technique, an effect replaces that essence's base effect, on every kind of form: on a struck foe (strikes, bolts, shouts, pulls, pushes, a dash's or a leap's foes, a mark, sight's foes) and on yourself (Within, or a Shell rising: the effect gathers around you), the lead at full strength, the second at half, the third at about a third. Unmaking's keep the small cost in health every Unmaking cast pays. What stays a while is drawn in code (burning floor, mist, rubble, blades, flowers, a thread, frost, a blast) and a new floor clears it; sounds come from the bank.

| Essence | Effect (opens) | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| Fire | Kindle (12) | leaves burning floor where it hits |
| Fire | Flare (36) | a second blast where it struck, half a second later |
| Fire | Smoulder (72) | sets a smoulder: when the foe falls, its burn jumps to the next (3 times) |
| Water | Chill (12) | chills; three chills in 4 s freeze it a moment |
| Water | Current (36) | carries what it hits along your aim (on yourself: carries you) |
| Water | Mist (72) | leaves a mist: foes inside lose sight of you |
| Air | Spark (12) | leaps to one more foe |
| Air | Lift (36) | a light foe floats, helpless (a heavy one only staggers) |
| Air | Gust (72) | throws foes back hard |
| Stone | Rubble (12) | leaves rubble that slows what walks through |
| Stone | Grit (36) | you take less damage for a few seconds (on yourself, longer, and a skin of stone) |
| Stone | Tremor (72) | a small ring through the floor where it lands, staggering |
| Iron | Shrapnel (12) | shards fly from the struck foe into those around it (they pierce) |
| Iron | Temper (36) | your weapon strikes hit harder for a moment (on yourself, longer, and stamina back) |
| Iron | Lodestone (72) | pulls the loose foes around it together |
| Blade | Rend (12) | a bleed that deepens with each hit (up to 5) |
| Blade | Shards (36) | blades on the floor cut what walks over them |
| Blade | Answer (72) | the next foe to swing at you is cut first |
| Making | Bloom (12) | a flower that heals you when you step on it (4 at most) |
| Making | Lifeline (36) | a thread that drinks from a foe into you for 3 s |
| Making | Second Wind (72) | stamina back for each foe hit |
| Unmaking | Unravel (12) | its armour stays stripped: every blow pierces (enemy.gd unravel_t) |
| Unmaking | Hollow (36) | it takes 25% more from everything for 4 s (enemy.gd hollow_t) |
| Unmaking | Fray (72) | an erosion that grows faster the longer it lasts |

**The Techniques tab** (`scripts/technique_tab.gd`): everything about the Unbound's techniques, in one place, as a tab of the climber screen beside **Body** (shown only while the climber is Unbound; a marked climber never sees it). **Six slots** along its top, one per body part with its mark button's key: choose one. Its **card** draws the technique in a small brass-bound glass **globe** (the form's shape inside; the part's essences poured in, the lead filling most of it, the others swirling thinner above; the variation's fitting on its spout: a flared nozzle for Wide, a three-pronged splitter for Split, a coil for Chain, a little clock for Delayed...) beside a **strain dial** (out of 100, red past 70), and says in plain words its name, what it does, "Echoes ..." when the mix comes close to a bond's spell, how it fills, what one cast strains and what boost spends, and the part's level. A part not yet at level 3 says how far it has to go, a young technique says it's young and what level 6 brings, with the way to the Body tab. Under the card: **Form** (the forms the part's tree has opened; locked ones dark with their level), **Effects** (a button for each essence in the part, leading first, with its effect: press for the next that has opened, or hear what opens next; the essences themselves are the part's own mix, and a line with a **Body tab** button says where to move them), **Variation** (None, then each one; not yours yet sit dark), the **name** (optional) and **Boost**, then **where each kind of banked essence pools** (routing). **Every press changes the slot at once** and it's carried straight away: there's no draft and no Set button, so nothing can be lost by choosing another slot or closing the book; a change that finds an echo is noted for the climber and said under the card. Techniques change **only on the train**, between climbs (as when they were built at the old essence bench); anywhere else the tab shows everything with its buttons off and a line saying so. Every button has the Entity's hover/hold help, the Entity explains the tab the first time it opens, and "How do things work here?" has "The Techniques tab?". It works with the mouse, the keys and a controller (the focus stays on what you pressed) and a tap, and fits a phone at every Interface size. (The old essence bench in the Workshop car and the Techniques page in your car are gone: this tab does what both did.)

**Marks' trees** (`scripts/tree.gd`): a bond's steps, 1 to 12. Steps 1 and 5 are its spells. **Steps 2 and 4 are forks:** today's spell, or the tower's alternative. **Step 3 is a choice of two Transformations** of the bond's first spell (it gains a new behaviour and gets much stronger): today's side (each has a name: Stormcaller's is **Branching Storm**) or the tower's other side (Stormcaller's **Living Bolt**). The bond's **passive grows to rank II at step 3 and rank III at step 5** (each rank's line is in the passive's data, `bonds.gd` "ranks"; the mark's page, its card in the book's Marks tab, its Abilities entry and the tree's step 3 and 5 buds show the rank and its line), and the great spell learned at step 5 comes with its **Apex** upgrade. A fork is **ready** once the mark reaches its step and both sides are written; until it's chosen, **today's side stays in use**, so a climber who never chooses plays as before. Once chosen, the other side is gone: a mark never goes back. A side not written yet makes the fork wait (today's side stays). **Old saves:** every fork a mark has already passed keeps today's side, recorded as chosen; forks still ahead open as the mark grows. A step-3 choice made before the Transformations keeps its side (today's side is side A, the other side B, by the same ids), and the next time the book's **Marks** tab opens it offers each such mark **one free re-pick**: take the other side, or keep yours (`tree.gd` REPICK: a version-1 tree flags them in "repick"; once used or kept the offer is gone). When a step brings a ready fork, the stair's banner says "or Ball Lightning: choose in your book".

**Steps 6 to 12** (`scripts/mark_ascent.gd`, every name, line and number; the full design, mark by mark, is the Bonded Marks doc's "Steps 6–12"): the same rhythm as an Unbound Node's levels 6 to 12. **Step 6:** the passive grows to **rank IV** (it raises numbers of ranks II and III: "rank4" "boost", read by `bonds.gd` passive_num once `bonds.gd` HELD says the climber's mark is at rank IV; main.gd sets it as the marks are given). **Steps 7, 9 and 11:** a fork of **two upgrades** to the mark's spells (each lists both sides of the earlier fork it improves, so it works whichever was taken); **step 8:** the **Apex+**, the great spell strengthened; **step 10:** a **second Transformation** of the first spell, one of two; **step 12:** the **Zenith**, the mark's greatest power (the Unbound capstone's counterpart): every spell of the mark also does something more (Crown of Storms, Sky Throne, Apex Predator, Leviathan Tide, Aegis Eternal, The Abyss Answers, Armory Unbound, Sunforged, The Living Script, Living Mountain, Mother of Venoms, Ghost of Cinders, Heart of the Sun, Wings of the Sun, Undying Flame, Kinlord, The Many-Formed, The Last Physician). These forks have no side in use until chosen (nothing changes until then), and choosing is for good. An upgrade's "mods" change its spells' numbers (`bond.gd` spell_stat: "+", "*" or "="; "*" is every spell of the mark); its "fx" are run as the spell is cast by `scripts/spells/ascent.gd` (an echo of the spell, a burst around you, strikes on the nearest foes, a meteor after a warning ring, circling blades, a rune circle, a wave of fire, a shell, healing, a surge of power, a spirit creature; each at most once every second or so, drawn with the shading toolkit, warnings flat). A mark holds 10 more essence a step to step 5 and 5 more a step after (115 at step 12); the creatures it calls count steps past 5 at half (`bond.gd` ally_step); reactions grow 5% a step past 5 instead of 15% (`reactions.gd` RULES "per_high_step"). The tree's high-step buds have hover help (`entity_help.gd` "bud_rank4", "bud_upgrade", "bud_apex", "bud_transform", "bud_zenith"; the Zenith wears a crown), the Entity explains step 6 and the Zenith the first time (`entity.gd` TIPS "mark_high", "mark_zenith"), and "How do marks grow?" covers them. **Pace** (`profile.gd` STEP_XP, the depth each step needs: 0, 170, 300, 420, 540, 820, 960, 1100, 1240, 1400, 1560, 1740; marks grow by use, `scripts/mark_use.gd`; held by `tests/parts/progression.gd` to the owner's targets): a typical climber's first mark reaches step 5 after about 6 climbs, step 6 after about 8 and step 12 after about 23 (about 9 summiting its tower every climb), the same pace as an Unbound Node to level 12. Old climbers keep every step: their progress becomes depth as they load.

**Stormcaller, the worked example** (`scripts/spells/gryphon.gd`): step 2, **Chain Lightning** or **Ball Lightning** (a slow orb drifts where you aim for 3.2 seconds, sparking each foe it passes for half its damage and charging it; every second lightning leaps from it to 2 foes, 3 at step 4, twice as many under Thunderhead; it stops at a wall; costs 10); step 3, a Transformation of Call Lightning: **Branching Storm** (today's side: the bolt lands on the foe in your aim as ever, then every leap splits in two, up to 6 foes, 12 under Thunderhead, each struck for the bolt's strike and left charged; a planted Lightning Rod is a stop on the way) or **Living Bolt** (Call Lightning as it was, then for 3 seconds the bolt stays alive on the foe it struck: every 0.3 seconds it leaps from the foe it holds to the nearest other charged foe within 220, striking it for 40% of the bolt's strike and keeping it charged); step 4, **Lightning Rod** or **Thunderclap** (a clap of thunder around you: every charged foe within 240 is struck for 8 and stunned for 1.5 seconds, 1.8 at step 5, and its charge is spent; foes close by that aren't charged are only shoved back; costs 10).

**The Gryphon's and the Kraken's other bonds** (`scripts/spells/gryphon.gd`, `scripts/spells/kraken.gd`; tests in `tests/parts/forks_gryphon_kraken.gd` and, for the Transformations, passive ranks and Apex upgrades, `tests/parts/marks_gryphon_kraken.gd`). Things that last a while draw themselves as small nodes under the SpellFx node, so they pause with the fight and a new floor clears them. A big beast is held only half as long by any of their stuns. Each passive's ranks and each great spell's Apex are in **Marks, essence and the Regard**.
- **Windwalker:** step 2, **Crosswind** or **Updraft** (a column of rising wind where you aim, for 4 seconds, 5 at step 4: each foe that walks into it is thrown into the air once; costs 9); step 3, **Skyward** (today's side: a 55° cone reaching 165 throws foes twice as long into the air, and each slams down where it lands, taking 10, while the foes near where it lands, within 70, take 6) or **Tailwind** (you ride the gust 190 forward in 0.3 seconds, untouchable; every foe in the gust or that you pass is thrown up for 1.75 times as long and struck for 8; the ride stops at a wall); step 4, **Downdraft** or **Hurl** (every foe in the air near you is flung along your aim, striking each foe in its path for 12 and landing hard for 15; costs 10).
- **Hunter:** step 2, **Track** or **Snare** (a loop on the floor where you aim; the first foe to step in it is stunned a moment, struck and slowed to a crawl for 3 seconds; two can wait at once, for 20 seconds; costs 6); step 3, **Seeking Shot** (today's side: a 0.45 s wind-up; the arrow curves toward your Quarry, or the foe you aim at, which becomes it, piercing what's in the way, and strikes the Quarry twice at double damage) or **Rending Shot** (the arrow pierces a whole line, and every foe it passes bleeds 4 every half second for 5 seconds); step 4, **Hawk's Eye** or **Pinning Shot** (a heavy arrow that pins the first foe it strikes for 2 seconds, open to ripostes; costs 10).
- **Tidecaller:** step 2, **Riptide** or **Flood** (a wide pool where you aim for 6 seconds: foes in it wade slowly and take a little damage; fliers pass over it; costs 9); step 3, **Breaking Wave** (today's side: the aimed wave, and 0.45 seconds later a second one from where you cast it, which catches the foes where the first threw them) or **Return Tide** (the burst around you, reaching 150; 0.6 seconds later every foe it struck is dragged back to one spot 75 in front of where you cast it, struck again, soaked and held 0.4 seconds); step 4, **Carry** or **Geysers** (three geysers burst one after another along your aim, each striking for 10 and staggering; costs 14).
- **Warden** (the proposal's): step 2, **Absorb** or **Bulwark** (a curved wall of water where you stand, across your aim, for 4 seconds, 5 at step 4: it stops the monsters' shots and holds back foes walking into it; costs 8); step 3, **Bastion** (today's side: half of every blow it blocks comes back to it, up to twice its strength, and it bursts with all it absorbed when it breaks, over 140) or **Moving Wall** (the barrier stands as a curved wall 52 out along your aim, 60° either side, and moves and turns with you while it holds: the monsters' shots that reach it fly back half again as hard, foes walking into it are shoved back, and blows from that side wear the barrier half as fast; unlike Bulwark, which stands where you raised it and only stops shots, it is the barrier itself); step 4, **Challenge** or **Tidebreak** (for 8 seconds every parry made while your barrier holds sends out a wave for 14; it raises a thin barrier if none holds; costs 10).
- **Deepcaller:** step 2, **Anchor** or **Leech** (clings to the foe you aim at for 6 seconds, taking 3 a second and giving you half; costs 8); step 3, **Abyssal Draw** (today's side: Undertow drains 5 foes within 250 at 135% and pulls each toward you, holding it half a second) or **Cold Current** (Undertow drains its 3 foes at 130%; they're slowed for 3 seconds, and twice the life you drink also becomes a shield for 6 seconds, on top of any you hold); step 4, **Siphon** or **Drown** (the foe you aim at takes 10 plus 30% of the health it has lost, at most 40 more, and you drink 40%; a foe below half health is held a moment; costs 10).

**The Firebird's and the Chimera's forks** (`scripts/spells/firebird.gd`, `scripts/spells/chimera.gd`, all written; tests in `tests/parts/forks_firebird_chimera.gd`, and the marks' growth in `tests/parts/marks_firebird_chimera.gd`). Each pair is today's side **or** the new one. Step 3 is a choice between two **Transformations** of the first spell (each much stronger than the old variations); an old save keeps its choice: whoever had today's side has the first, whoever had the other side has the second.
- **Flame Contractor:** Fire Wall or **Blazing Trail** (for 4 seconds, 5 at step 4, you leave burning ground wherever you walk; costs 10); **Sunfire** (a slower, larger fireball, 250 px a second instead of 420, with a 95 px blast that hits half again as hard, that leaves your burning ground every 24 px of its flight for 3 seconds) or **Seeker Flame** (the fireball turns toward the nearest foe ahead of it, within 240, and where it bursts it bursts again 0.35 s later at 80%); Cluster Blast or **Detonate** (every patch of your burning ground, from Fire Wall, Blazing Trail, Sunfire or Kindled, bursts at once, up to 6 bursts of 14, 17 at step 5; with none, one bigger burst at your aim; costs 12).
- **Flamewing:** Wingburst or **Cinder Dive** (you dive 150 px along your aim through foes, striking each for 8 plus a third of the aura's heat and setting it burning; free in Firebird Form; costs 10); **Furnace** (Heat Aura's damage climbs to two and a half times over 4 seconds, where Growing doubled over 10, its heat builds twice as fast, and foes in it are slowed) or **Ember Wings** (the aura grows hotter as Growing did, and while it burns it reaches out as two wings to your sides, across your aim, out to 2.3 times its reach: foes they touch burn at one and a half times the aura's damage and are knocked away, each at most once a second); Warmth or **Heat Haze** (for 8 seconds the aura burns away shots that enter it and sets the foes in it burning; it lights the aura if it's off; costs 10).
- **Rekindler:** Desperation or **Flashpoint** (spend a tenth of your health, never the last, in a burst of fire around you, up to two and a half times harder the lower your health; costs 9); **Bonfire** (while you burn, the foes you strike catch fire, and each one, up to 5, adds half of what you lost to the payback) or **Cauterize** (Rekindle heals a tenth of your health at once, and its payback comes as a barrier of three times what you lost, for 10 seconds, instead of health); Pyre Oath or **Backdraft** (for 10 seconds any blow that takes 3 or more health bursts into fire around you; costs 12).
- **Covenant:** Command or **Rally** (your creatures heal a third and stay 4 seconds longer, and for 8 seconds hit a third again as hard (35%) and move 30% faster; costs 6); **Pack Call** (each Call brings two creatures at 85% of their strength that hunt as a pack: each blow lands 50% harder for every packmate within 150; never more than three out) or **Champion Bond** (Call brings one champion twice as strong, crowned, with no longer wind-up; every kill it makes this floor grows it by a quarter, up to two and a half times, and a champion called later on the floor starts that big; binding is unchanged); Exchange or **Release** (your nearest creature bursts, hitting foes around it for 12 plus 40% of its health left, and 30% of that health heals you; costs 8).
- **Grafter:** Mimic or **Harvest** (a cut for 14, 18 at step 4; a foe below a third of its health is slain and always leaves a graft; costs 9); **Hungry Flesh** (each graft adds its own effect to Graft Burst, and the burst tears a graft from the weakest foe it caught below half health: that foe is struck again and the graft binds to you at once if its part is free) or **Flesh Shell** (the burst grows a shell of 16 plus 6 for every graft you carry, for 6 seconds); Shed or **Bud** (your newest graft grows into a creature of that monster's kind for 15 seconds, one at a time; the graft stays; with no graft, a lump of your own flesh buds as a slime for 40% of the time; costs 10).
- **Surgeon:** Mend or **Bloodletting** (a cut that bleeds the foe for 6 seconds, 2.5 every half second, rising with steps, and heals you for 40%; costs 10); **Vivisect** (Sever finds a weak point: your next 3 hits on that foe within 8 seconds are critical, two and a half times as hard) or **Arterial Cut** (Sever bleeds hard: half its damage every half second instead of 15%); Transfer or **Anesthetize** (the foe sleeps for 4 seconds, a beast much less; the blow that wakes it strikes half again as hard; bleeding and burning don't wake it; costs 10).

**Passive ranks.** A mark's passive is rank I from the start, grows to **II** at step 3 and **III** at step 5 (`bonds.gd` `passive_rank`; each passive's `"ranks"` lines and numbers are in its bond, in the Data editor). The mark's page in the book, its card, the Abilities cards and the Tree show the rank and what each rank adds. A Transformation's first side runs in its tower file's `cast_change` (`library.gd` `cast_mark_change`), its second in `cast_variation`.

Firebird Form makes any spell marked `"free_in_form": true` free (Wingburst, and Cinder Dive too), at most once every 1.5 seconds (its `free_every`). Rekindle's payback is player.gd's (Bonfire's growth and Cauterize's barrier too). What these spells leave in the world is drawn by `spell_fx.gd`'s shared lasting helper (`firebird.gd spawn(main, seconds, tick, paint, data)` passes it on), which a new floor clears along with the library's spell effects.

**The Wyvern's and the Manticore's forks** (`scripts/spells/wyvern.gd`, `scripts/spells/manticore.gd`), each side costing what today's does at that step or close to it:
- **Blade Forger:** step 2, **Recall** or **Tethered Blade** (one heavy blade on an iron chain skewers the first foe in its path, staggers it and drags it to you; a beast is only struck; a heavier weapon hits harder; costs 9); step 4, **Bladeward** or **Falling Swords** (three great swords hang over the foe you aim at and fall one after another, 16 each and a stagger; costs 14).
- **Flameblade:** step 2, **Flame Lash** or **Flame Wheel** (a burning spin: every foe around you is struck for 9 and set burning, and each one heats your weapon; costs 9); step 4, **Searing Parry** or **Blazing Lunge** (you lunge 150 px through foes, untouchable, burning each one for 15, and your weapon gains 30 heat; costs 10).
- **Rune Artist:** step 2, **Inscribe** or **Rune Brand** (a rune on the foe you aim at bursts for 16 against it and the foes beside it the moment it's hurt by anything; costs 8); step 4, **Ward Circle** or **Binding Circle** (a circle at your aim that foes can walk into but not out of for 4 seconds; a beast is only chilled; costs 14). The bench and the camps answer to the Rune Artist's mark, not to Inscribe, so its crafting works whichever side it takes.
- **Sandshaper:** step 2, **Sand Wall** or **Sandblast** (a cone of sand stings the foes in front for 7 and blinds them for 2.5 seconds; costs 9); step 4, **Sinkhole** or **Quicksand** (a wide patch at your aim for 6 seconds: foes in it are slowed hard, drawn to its middle and take 3 every half second; costs 14).
- **Venomist:** step 2, **Toxic Cloud** or **Contagion** (the foe you aim at takes 2 stacks, and for 6 seconds every foe near it takes a stack every half second; costs 10); step 4, **Neurotoxin** or **Enfeeble** (every poisoned foe near you hits 40% softer, a beast 20%, and walks slower for 6 seconds; costs 10).
- **Ashwalker:** step 2, **Afterimage** or **Ash Step** (you step 135 px along your aim at once, leaving a smoke cloud where you stood whose foes lose you; costs 9); step 4, **Vanish** or **Cinder Trail** (for 5 seconds your steps leave patches of your own fire that burn foes; costs 12).

**How the Wyvern's and the Manticore's marks grow** (step 3's Transformations, the passive's ranks, the great spell's Apex). Step 3 is a choice between two **Transformations** of the first spell: it gains a new behaviour and gets much stronger. Side A takes the place of the old step-3 change, side B the place of the other old side, and both keep the old choice ids, so an old save that chose the old change has side A and one that chose the other side has side B (`back_to_back`, `ring_of_embers`, `afterglow`, `aftershock`, `spray` and `billowing` are now side B's ids). The passive grows to **rank II at step 3** and **rank III at step 5** (`bonds.gd` passive "ranks", `passive_rank`), and the step-5 spell has its **Apex** (its "apex" line and numbers in its step 5). The mark's page, the Abilities cards and the tree show the rank and its new line. Side A's numbers are the first spell's step 3 in `bonds.gd`; side B's are `FORKS[bond][3]` in the tower's file, which runs them all.
- **Blade Forger.** Step 3: **Embedded Steel** (one blade that hits stays in each foe and bursts 0.6 seconds later for the blade's damage against it and a quarter of that against every foe within 36) or **Weapon Echo** (five blades, each carrying your weapon's finisher: fists, maces, spears, greatswords, war hammers, canes and crossbows stagger, a dagger poisons for 3 stacks, a sword, axe, sword-cane or shortbow leaves bleeding and a pistol burns, each half second for a fifth to a third of the blade's damage; the table is `echo` in `wyvern.gd`). Weapon Memory **II**: blades carry your weapon's quality (rarity and temper, as your strikes do) and its traits (of Embers, a venomous blade's poison). **III**: every 5th melee strike that lands throws a free blade of Conjured Blades (with its Transformation). **Apex**, Thousand Edges: each blade leaves 3 edges along the first half of its path for 4 seconds; a foe on one is cut for 2 and bleeds, every half second.
- **Flameblade.** Step 3: **Forge Heart** (while kindled, heat never fades and climbs past full to 150, so strikes hit up to 75% harder; Kindle Blade lasts 8 seconds) or **Brand** (kindled strikes brand foes for 8 seconds; when the kindling goes out, or you cast Kindle Blade again or Overheat, your heat is released: every branded foe ignites for 8 plus 0.2 per point of heat, half that to the foes touching it, and burns; the heat is spent if anything ignited). Heat **II**: at full heat every swing throws a short wave of fire (6, and burning). **III**: a parry adds 25 heat, a perfect one 45. **Apex**, Overheat: released at 100 heat or more, it also erupts in a ring of fire (16 within 110, and burning) and lights Kindle Blade again.
- **Rune Artist.** Step 3: **Linked Runes** (setting off one rune sets off every rune, and fire runs from each to the next, round in a ring: each foe on the way is struck once for 22 and set burning, and the fire stays on the floor along the lines for 4 seconds, burning foes, not you) or **Creeping Runes** (runes crawl 70 px a second toward the nearest foe within 300, and burst wider, 75, and half again as hard). Runesmith **II**: a camp's rune comes with a second, different rune of your choice (both last the climb). **III**: spells you cast inside your circles (a Ward Circle, a Binding Circle, the Grand Sigil) cost 40% less. **Apex**, Grand Sigil: it moves with you for the first half of its time.
- **Sandshaper.** Step 3: **Shatter** (the quake leaves sharp shards over its ground for 5 seconds; a foe walking over them is cut for a quarter of the quake, at most once a second) or **Upheaval** (the quake throws every foe it strikes into the air for 1.6 seconds, helpless; a beast is only rocked). Shifting Sands **II**: slowed foes (the slowed state, your rubble, Shifting Sands' last steps) can't block or shrug off your blows: no shell or guard holds. **III**: standing still for a second hardens your skin: you take 30% less damage. **Apex**, Fault Line: every foe on the line as it splits is buried (stunned 2 seconds, a beast 1) and crushed for 28.
- **Venomist.** Step 3: **Corrosive** (the spit still pierces; your poison strips shells and guards, and a poisoned foe takes 25% more from blows) or **Bloom** (Venom Spit bursts into a small cloud of poison where it lands, 45 wide for 2.5 seconds). Toxin Build **II**: a poisoned foe that dies passes half its stacks (at least one) to each foe within 90. **III**: a foe at 8 stacks or more is staggered for 0.8 seconds (a beast half), at most once every 4 seconds. **Apex**, Virulence: each foe it bursts gives the mark back half a Venom Spit's essence.
- **Ashwalker.** Step 3: **Ember Smoke** (the smoke burns the foes inside it, 2 every half second, 3 at step 5) or **Shroud Walk** (the cloud moves with you for its first 3 seconds). Unseen Strike **II**: an unseen strike also knocks the foe off balance for 0.6 seconds, breaking off its attack (a flinch, not the stun a riposte needs). **III**: a kill while unseen hides you again for 1.5 seconds. **Apex**, Ashfall: while it falls, an unseen strike leaps to the nearest other foe within 160 for the same damage.

Tests: `tests/parts/marks_wyvern_manticore.gd`.

What these spells leave in the world for a while (chains, hanging swords, brands, circles, quicksand, cracks) is drawn by each file's own small node under main ("WyvernFx", "ManticoreFx"), which forgets it all when a new floor starts, as `spell_fx.gd` does. Tests: `tests/parts/forks_wyvern_manticore.gd`.

**The Unbound's trees:** one per Node, a node at each level that brings something (12 levels by size, waking at 1): **level 1** the minor ability (waking); **3** a young technique (the first starting form); **5** the gift; **6** the full technique (the other starting form, the other essences); **7, 9 and 11** a fork of two variations; **8 and 10** a new form opens (each part's two are below); **12** (full) the capstone (below), with the signature of a pure part beside it. The forks per part are in `scripts/spells/unbound_forms.gd` (`TREE`). **Effects** open as their essence fills the body (the six parts' fill of that kind: 12, 36 and 72), shown on the tree of the part that essence pools in naturally (the path's essence on the heart's); all 24 are written (below). A chosen effect replaces its essence's base effect in a technique (a technique's `effects`). The Unbound keep what they choose, but once the part is **one level past** a fork they can **learn the side they passed on**, for **20 + 10 × the fork's level** crystal essence (any kinds, the biggest crystal first: `tree.gd SKIP_COST`). The techniques page has a **Variation** button once they own one (one variation per technique for now, `Library.MAX_VARIATIONS`), and the part button also cycles through the forms their tree has opened.

**The Unbound's new forms and capstones** (`scripts/spells/unbound_forms.gd`, every name, line and number in its `FORMS` and `TREE`). Each part's tree opens two new forms, at levels 8 and 10 (each adds strain on top of a technique's: 2 for a level-8 form, 4 for a level-10 one). **Hand:** **Volley** (four small bolts of 7 in a fan; they bend a little toward a foe ahead within 150, and each that strikes a foe the same volley already struck hits 30% harder for every earlier hit, so a volley on one foe adds up) and **Brand** (a touch marks the foe in front; 0.8 s later it bursts around it, or on the floor if no one was there). **Legs:** **Stride** (for 3 seconds you're quickened and leave a glowing trail; foes on it take the effect every half second) and **Vault** (a hop backwards, untouchable for 0.6 seconds, with a burst of 18 where you stood; the burst stays on the ground and bursts twice more, half a second apart, at 40%, each with the technique's effect). **Back:** **Wake** (a slow ring rolls out from you, striking each foe once and pushing it) and **Mantle** (a light shell and a cloak of light on your back; the next blow you take, on the shell or on you, is answered with a burst that throws foes off). **Throat:** **Hum** (a field around you that pulses three times, half a second apart, and moves with you) and **Call** (a burst far away where you aim, on the first foe near your aim if there is one). **Brow:** **Glare** (a narrow beam you hold for a second; it turns with your aim, stops at walls, damages every 0.2 s and brings the essences' effects every third tick) and **Omen** (a sign on the floor a step ahead that waits 10 seconds and bursts when a foe steps on it). **Heart:** **Heartbeat** (the effect beats out around you every second, four times) and **Wellspring** (a pool at your feet for 7 seconds; while you stand in it, it works on you as Within does, a tick at a time). Every hit goes through the technique's essences (lead, second, third, chosen effects), Air stretches each form's reach, and every variation works on each: Wide and Long widen and stretch it (Long also lengthens Stride, Mantle and Wellspring and widens the rings), Quick, Charged, Echo, Delayed, Chain and Lingering as on any technique, and a form centred on you (Wake, Hum, Heartbeat, Wellspring) under Split or Mirrored is cast a step away along each copy's aim, so Split fans three smaller copies out in front and Mirrored puts one behind you. **Capstones** (level 12, a part full; strong, but each plays through strain): **Raw Hands** (every hand technique also throws raw bolts at up to three foes near you, five while the hand runs hot), **Afterimage** (every legs technique leaves an afterimage where you stood that bursts a moment later, harder the hotter your legs run), **Unbowed** (every back technique also raises a light shell, and while any shell holds you, every part's strain fades, even as you cast), **Rallying Cry** (every throat technique fills your other techniques by a quarter, cools their parts by 10 strain and gives back your stamina), **Piercing Gaze** (every brow technique marks every foe near you for 4 seconds, 6 while the brow runs hot: they take 50% more damage) and **Second Heart** (once a floor, below a third of your health, 40% of it back and every part's strain gone). Their numbers are in each capstone's entry in `TREE`. The file runs them itself with one node under the game (`UnboundForms`, which also draws the forms' beams, rings, trails, signs, pools and cloaks in code); it hears each cast through `Library.on_cast`. Tests: `tests/parts/unbound_forms.gd`.

**The book** (`scripts/book.gd`): the climber screen is a bound grimoire, drawn in code: a leather cover tooled in gilt, an open spread of candlelit parchment (dark vellum, so the menus' light ink reads on it), the spine's crease, and a silk **ribbon** for each section (Abilities gold, Tree green, Marks crimson, Essence blue, Record violet, Core silver; colours in the Data editor's "The climber's book"). Pages turn with the ribbons, **Q** / **E**, **LB** / **RB** or a swipe across the page, and a soft shadow sweeps across as they do. It opens from your name on the rail, **Tab**, the **Climber** button, and **the book lying in Your Car** on the train (a thick volume on a cushion, beside the Inventory and Appearance; a gilt mote glows over it while a choice waits). It reflows at every Interface size like every page.

**The Tree section** (`scripts/tree_view.gd`, where all its drawing lives): the body map. Your climber stands on the left (the same figure as the Marks tab) with a **node on each body part**; a part with a ready fork pulses gold. Tap or select a part and its tree grows out of the figure (see The art of the tree and the book, below): a node for each step or level, a **bud** for each option ("or" between a fork's two). Buds show what each piece is to you: **yours**, **in use until you choose** (today's side of a ready fork), a **ready choice** (pulsing gold), **locked** (a lock; the card says what opens it), **passed on** (faded, struck through), **learnable again** (violet: the Unbound) and **Coming soon** (dashed). Select a bud for its **grimoire card** below, and **Choose ..., for good**: it asks first (a pop-up: "Choose Ball Lightning?", **Confirm** chooses, **Cancel** doesn't). The Entity explains that choices are for good the first time a fork is ready. The Unbound's passed sides have **Learn ... · N crystal essence**, which ask first too. Unbound trees list the part's **Essences** under it.

**The grimoire card** (`scripts/grimoire_card.gd`): one card for every entry, the same in Abilities, on the Tree and later on the Techniques tab: a small drawing (the medallion, or a glyph of what it does: a cone, a fan of bolts, a ring...), the name and a tag, the one plain line, a line of numbers, how it's cast (its key) and what it costs, an "echoes" line, and "+ more" for the full numbers.

**The art of the tree and the book** (all drawn in code: `tree_view.gd`, `book.gd`, `grimoire_card.gd`, the window's star in `train_view.gd`). **The body map:** the chosen part's tree grows out of the climber like a mark spreading: a branch in the bond's colour leaves the mark (little veins spreading round it), crosses to a **root** bearing the bond's emblem and runs down as a trunk, a node on it for each step (its number in a disc: filled once grown, gold and pulsing when a fork waits, dark while locked, dotted for what's to come), each option a **bud** on a twig, a fork's two sides on two twigs with "or" between. The mark's emblem is echoed along the grown branch (Air sparks, Water ripples, Fire flicks, Stone chips, Iron rivets, Blade thorns). For the Unbound the branch is a **vein of raw essence** in the part's colour that flickers hot with strain (sparks run along it past 30). Buds: yours glow, passed-on ones fade and are struck through, a ready choice pulses gold, a locked one shows a lock and what opens it ("at step 4"), a passed Unbound side what learning it costs, "Coming soon" is faint and dashed; each has the glyph its data suggests. Everything comes from the tree's data, so new content shows by itself. What doesn't move is drawn once; only the glows are drawn each frame. Side by side on a computer and a phone at Normal; at a bigger Interface size the tree goes under the figure. **Reaching it:** tap a part, then a bud; with the keys or a controller, up and down the parts, **Right** onto the branch (on the chosen bud), up and down along it, Left and Right between a node's buds, Left off it back to the part, **A** or **Enter** to read a bud (and its card's Choose). **Links:** every Abilities entry has **On the body map** (the Tree opens with its bud chosen and glowing a moment), and a bud's card has **In your abilities** (back to that entry, picked out and scrolled to). **The window's star:** while a choice waits, a new star shines in a window of Your Car (the first spot clear of the car's things, the moon and the towers); tap or click it and the book opens on the Tree, on that part. The book on its cushion keeps its gilt mote, for the keys and the controller. **The book:** a double gilt fillet with stamped dots between, a fleuron in each corner, striped headbands at the spine's ends and gilt page edges; satin ribbons with a sheen and a gilt bar where they leave the spine; turning a page lifts a leaf that sweeps over the spine, a shadow running ahead of it (a third of a second). **Glyphs** (`grimoire_card.gd glyph_for`): a glyph made for each of today's forms, variations and effects and for the spell types where it matters (Ball Lightning an orb, Lightning Rod a rod); anything else is read from its data: the words of its line ("a wall of wind" is a wall, "three small bolts in a fan" a fan, `WORDS`), then its numbers (a radius is a ring, a speed a bolt, `NUMBERS`), then its class or essence. A spell's glyph sits on a disc of its colour with its bond's emblem as a small seal. Tests: `tests/parts/art.gd`.

**Dev tools** (the dev page's "The tree"): how many pieces you own and choices are ready, **Forget every choice** (to try the forks again) and **Open the Tree**.

### Adding spells: the content contract

Several people can add spells at once without touching each other's files. Each tower has its own file, **`scripts/spells/<tower>.gd`** (gryphon, kraken, wyvern, manticore, firebird, chimera), and the Unbound have **`scripts/spells/unbound_forms.gd`** and **`scripts/spells/unbound_effects.gd`**. `library.gd` loads them all when it starts and merges what they declare; nothing else needs registering. The contract is also written at the top of `library.gd`.

**A tower's forks** (`static var FORKS := {bond_id: {...}}`), for each of its bonds:

```gdscript
"stormcaller": {
	"change": {"name": "Branching Storm", "says": "the bolt lands, then every leap splits in two..."},  # today's side of the step-3 choice, named
	2: {"id": "ball_lightning", "name": "Ball Lightning", "type": "ball_lightning", "learn": 2,
		"desc": "a slow orb of lightning drifts where you aim...",
		"steps": {2: {"cost": 10.0, "windup": 0.35, "damage": 6.0, ...}, 4: {"damage": 8.0}}},  # as in bonds.gd
	3: {"id": "lingering", "name": "Living Bolt", "desc": "...",
		"steps": {3: {"live": 3.0, "beat": 0.3, "live_power": 0.4, "live_hop": 220.0}}},  # replaces today's step-3 values
	4: {"id": "thunderclap", "name": "Thunderclap", "desc": "...", "placeholder": true, "learn": 4},  # not written yet
},
```

and the code that runs them:

- `static func cast_spell(bond, sp: Dictionary, p) -> bool`: runs your spell types (`sp["type"]`, unique in the game); true if handled. Use the bond's helpers as `bond.gd`'s own spells do: `bond.stat(key)`, `bond.damage()`, `bond.color()`, `bond.spell_power(sp)`, and the player `p` (`p.main`, `p.aim`, `p.global_position`).
- `static func cast_change(bond, sp, p) -> bool` (optional): your own way to run today's side of the step-3 choice from step 3 on (a Transformation's side A; its numbers are the first spell's step 3 in `bonds.gd`). Return false and the spell casts as before.
- `static func cast_variation(bond, sp, p, alt) -> bool` (optional): your own way to run a step-3 variation (call `bond._cast_type(p)` for the spell itself). Return false and the library runs it generically: Split, Mirrored, Echo and Delayed cast the spell again at their power; Wide, Long, Quick, Charged and Chain change its numbers (`library.gd mark_stat`).
- Things that stay a while (orbs, fields, timed casts) go through `scripts/spell_fx.gd`: `later(main, seconds, fn)`, `field(main, pos, radius, seconds, tick, col, fn)`, `orb(main, pos, vel, seconds, radius, col, fn)`, `mark(main, pos, seconds, col)`; and anything with its own drawing, `lasting(main, seconds, tick, paint, data)` (a small node: `tick(n, delta)`, `paint(n)` with `n.draw_*`, `n.fade()`, `n.finish()`) or `item(main, seconds, tick, paint, high)` (a dictionary painted into a `draw_util.gd` Batch). A new floor clears them all.
- **States and reactions** (`scripts/reactions.gd`; see **States, reactions and synergies**): `Reactions.apply(main, foe, state, seconds, source, amount := 0.0)` gives a foe a state (`"charged"`, `"airborne"`, `"soaked"`, `"burning"`, `"poisoned"`, `"bleeding"`, `"slowed"`, `"unseen"`) or a moment (`"slam"`, `"drained"`) and returns the ids of the reactions it set off (`[]` if none); `amount` is burning or bleeding damage each half second or poison stacks (0: the state's own; -1: only the state, when your spell does its own damage). `Reactions.has(foe, state)`, `Reactions.left(foe, state)`, `Reactions.held(foe)`, `Reactions.clear(foe, state)`, `Reactions.num(id, key)`. Listen with `main.on_reaction(foe, id, source)` (or a foe's `on_reaction`), or `static func on_reaction(p, info)` in your file (`info`: id, foe, at, source, the states it held). A foe's old fields still work and still react: `e.charged = 4.0`, `e.ignite(dmg, s)`, `e.add_venom(n)`, `e.lift(s)`, `e.lose_track(s, null)`, `e.chill_t = s`, and the new `e.soak(s)`, `e.bleed(dps, s)`. A new state or reaction is a row in `STATES` or `REACTIONS` (and its effect in `reactions.gd _fire`). Give every spell the states it should leave, so reactions happen without anyone planning them.
- **Synergies** (`scripts/synergies.gd`): `Synergies.has(p, id)`, `Synergies.held(p)`, `Synergies.num(id, key)`, `Synergies.lent(p, bond_id)`; a new named synergy is a row in `SYNERGIES` (its two `bonds`, name, line, colour, numbers) and a `Synergies.has(...)` check where it acts; a mark's lent trait is its row in `TRAITS` and a case in `_once` (casting) or `_hit` (hits and kills). The file's header lists where each one acts.
- Hooks to listen to instead of watching numbers: `static func on_cast(p, cast)` in your file hears every cast (a technique's or a mark's spell: `Library.on_cast`); a foe's `on_hit(foe, dmg, source)` signal (and `main.on_hit` for any foe) when it loses health; the climber's `on_parry(source, perfect)`; `spell_fx.gd listen(node, signal, fn)` listens while your lasting node lives. To soften or harden a foe's blows, scale its `dmg_mult`, never `dmg`.

**New Unbound forms** (`unbound_forms.gd`: `static var FORMS`, as in `library.gd FORMS`, with `"part"`, `"class"`, `"says"`, numbers, `"strain"` and `"opens"` = the level) run in `static func cast_form(id, t, p, power, run, sh, w) -> int` (return the foes touched, -1 if it isn't yours; `sh` is the form's numbers with any variation applied, `w` the essences' weights; `Technique.hit(p, e, dmg, dir, w, power, run)` strikes a foe with every essence's effect, `run.add_bolt(...)` throws a bolt).
